## 2. Executive summary

The project is in good shape where it matters most: no critical defect, the release 1.3.0 is what its record says (tag, hash and checksum checked against
GitHub), the app's hard rules hold (no `INTERNET`, no GMS, minimal permissions, AGPL headers, no scanning), every timing constant matches the code, and every
census number of `ai-sessions/0090` reproduced exactly when re-run. What needs work is **accuracy of the record** and three **app defects**.

**69 findings** (0 critical, **7 major**, 50 minor, 12 nit) plus 5 protocol leads. The seven major ones:

1. **A93-PROT-01** — the 🟢 FACT on setting 13 (`PROTOCOL.md` §4.1) states "73 of 73 … for every mode change" and "12 of 77"; re-derived: 75 of 89 Buds-side
   changes were pushed, 12 of 89 lost to the Buds' `DISC`. The wrong ratio reached ADR-064 and the 1.3.0 release notes.
2. **A93-CAP-05** — `PROTOCOL.md` and `CAP-058` call the link keys P-256; the HCI event says P-192 (`CAP-058` 2235).
3. **A93-APP-01** — "Use different Buds" clears the choice on a second, Activity-owned `BudsCompanionPairing`; the repository's singleton still connects to the old
   Buds by name.
4. **A93-ARCH-01** — `ARCHITECTURE.md` §3.1's ANC row still describes the removed 1.2.x hold and the cause reset at Connect as current.
5. **A93-CAP-06** — Test-IDs swapped in five capture notes (e.g. `ANC-002`/`ANC-004` in `CAP-068`) — the Test-ID → capture → frame chain points to the wrong evidence.
6. **A93-CAP-08 / A93-CAP-09** — capture FINDINGS with conclusions resting on false premises (`CAP-007`'s "184 s silence on DLCI 0x08", `CAP-029`'s invented
   factory-reset precedent, `CAP-040`'s dock column, `CAP-021`'s "re-affirm" that is a real write, `CAP-002`/`CAP-004`'s uniqueness claim).

The two `CAP-076` defects are confirmed in the code with exact lines and fixes (A93-APP-02, A93-APP-03), plus a related wait that a stream push can end
(A93-APP-04). Recurring patterns: arithmetic slips repeated across documents ("thirteen requests" in six files, A93-CAP-01), status lines and INDEX cells that
lag their RESULTs (A93-HK-05, A93-SES-06/07), and the big documents (`PROTOCOL.md` 3,771 lines, `REVERSE_ENGINEERING.md` 4,606) whose current truth is spread
over dated Updates. New on the protocol side: `CAP-021`'s audio waves are assistant sessions (L93-1), the SASS codes are defined by the official page (A93-PROT-03/04
— and "in-use" is in `0x41`, not `0x42`), the ATT `0x0044` item can be closed (A93-PROT-07), and setting 23 carries a field this app version's schema does not have (L93-4).

## 3. Method

- **Reading.** In full: the 19 documents of the prompt plus `README.md`, `DECISIONS.md`, `ai-sessions/INDEX.md`, `id_registry.csv`, `reverse-engineering/APK_VERSIONS.md`,
  `reverse-engineering/tools/BACKLOG.md`, the RESULTs of `0090`, `0091`, `0092`, the PROMPT of `0091`, `CAP-076-FINDINGS.md`. The PROMPTs of `0090`/`0092` and
  `CAP-076-EVENT-NOTES.md` were read for scope only (their RESULT/FINDINGS were the subject). Not read by the main session: the 150 other capture notes and
  sessions `0069` … `0089` (sub-reviews, below); the Kotlin sources outside the lines quoted in §4 (sub-reviews).
- **Re-derivations (M).** The `0090` census (`scripts/anc_setting13_crosscheck.py --census`, 54 logs); a scripted check of every hex citation with a frame number in
  `PROTOCOL.md`/`DECISIONS.md`/`ARCHITECTURE.md` (47); an ATT `0x0044` scan over all 103 logs; the Capture Index ↔ registry ↔ folder check (76 rows); ADR statuses vs
  the registry (65); §3.2's constants vs the code; the release facts via `gh` (tag, PR, assets, the published `.sha256`); targeted `tshark`/`pwrpc_decode.py` checks
  quoted in each finding. `scripts/__pycache__` stayed unchanged (`git status --short scripts` empty after every run).
- **Gate (Phase 0).** One run, green — the counts are in Phase 0 above.
- **External sources (fetched 2026-10-15).** developers.google.com/nearby/fast-pair/specifications/extensions/sass; code.claude.com/docs/en/setup. Two further
  references are named in findings as "not fetched this session".
- **Sub-reviews.** Seven read-only sub-reviews started 11:10 were all cut off by usage limits and returned nothing usable. Six new ones (16:00–19:45) returned full
  reports, written progressively to scratch files: captures 001–025, 026–050, 051–075; sessions 0069–0089; `:data`; `:hardware`/`:domain`/`:app`/`:ui`/CI. Their raw
  notes are Appendix B. Every item used in §4 was re-checked by the main session (marked M) or taken over after a check of its quoted lines/command (S); items not
  re-checked one by one are grouped and said to be so.
- **Deviations from the prompt.** The phases were not strictly sequential: phases 4–9 ran while the phase-3 and phase-7 sub-reviews were running, and §4 was
  reordered afterwards; the session moved from Fable 5.1 to Opus 5.5 after a usage limit (no loss — the state was in this file). The newer APK was not decompiled
  (Checkpoint 0). No file other than this RESULT was changed; the INDEX row of 0093 was left as the prompt-writing session wrote it.

### Phase 2 — Protocol (`PROTOCOL.md` read in full; the 0090 census re-run; every hex citation checked)

**A93-PROT-01 — The 2026-10-14 FACT on setting 13 counts a class against itself ("73 of 73") and uses a wrong denominator ("12 of 77").**
- Location: `PROTOCOL.md:856–862` — "receives a `SERVER_STREAM` `4:{13:n}` **for every mode change the Buds report** on the Message Stream: 73 of 73 Buds-side
  changes … Not pushed: a change the Buds make together with their `DISC` of MAESTRO (12 of 77 …)"; the same figures in `PROTOCOL.md:3767` (§8 row),
  `ai-sessions/0090_CROSSCHECK_RESULT_2026_10_14.md:14–16`, `CHANGELOG.md` `[1.3.0]` Known issues ("12 of 77 such changes"), `CAP-076-FINDINGS.md` §6.1
  ("the 12-of-77 `DISC` case").
- Category: error (arithmetic) / ambiguity in a 🟢 FACT. Label: 🟢 FACT for the counts below.
- Verified (M): re-run `python3 scripts/anc_setting13_crosscheck.py --census` over the 54 `btsnoop` logs of the 46 subscribing official-app captures
  (`CAP-049`'s combined log left out, as 0090 did): `39 notify-set: stream-same`, `73 notify-unprovoked: stream-same`, `2 … stream-other`, `2 … none, other`,
  `12 … none, Buds close MAESTRO within 0.1 s`, `100 stream: with a Notify within 1 s`, `3 stream: no Notify, no Message Stream open`, `359 / 17 / 6` for
  `ReadSetting 4:13` — every published number reproduced exactly (`scripts/__pycache__` unchanged). The Buds-side ("unprovoked") Notifies are **89** = 73 + 2 + 2
  + 12. "73 of 73" is the class that was *defined* as "a stream packet followed" (circular); "12 of 77" divides by the 77 changes that were **not** missed.
- Recommendation (a proposal for the maintainer; the FACT itself — setting 13 is the mode and is pushed to a subscriber — is not in doubt): "… 75 of 89
  Buds-side changes were pushed within 1 s (73 with the predicted value, 2 as two close changes matched crosswise); 12 of 89 were not, because the Buds closed
  MAESTRO 2–30 ms after the Notify; 2 of 89 fell on a re-open or a fresh Message Stream open". Correct the same figure in the §8 row and the `[1.3.0]`
  known issue (by a dated note; the release text itself is history). Severity: **major** (a 🟢 FACT's headline count misstates its own evidence, and the
  wrong ratio reached the user-facing release notes).

**A93-PROT-02 — "41 of 41 holds … while Play held the Message Stream" conflates the two users.**
- Location: `PROTOCOL.md:868–870` ("the Buds pushed every reported change of setting 13 to it, also while Google Play services held the Message Stream and
  received the `Notify` (41 of 41 holds shown …)"), `PROTOCOL.md:3768` (§8 row "41 of 41 holds pushed while Play held the Message Stream"); origin
  `CAP-076-FINDINGS.md` §13's proposal text.
- Category: error (conflation). Label: 🟢. Verified (M): `CAP-076-FINDINGS.md` §4.2 — 29 holds in part 1 (Owner, Play held DLCI 0x04) and 12 in part 2
  (secondary user, no Play — no client held the Message Stream; `CAP-076` claims table: Play 0 in H2L/H2).
- Recommendation: "29 of 29 while Play held the Message Stream (part 1), 12 of 12 without Play (part 2)". Severity: minor.

**A93-PROT-03 — The SASS burst's "in-use" string is attributed to Code `0x42`; it is in `0x41`.**
- Location: `PROTOCOL.md:1706–1708` — "Group `0x07` (Codes `0x11`/`0x21`/`0x34`/`0x40`/`0x41`/`0x42`, the last containing the ASCII string `"in-use"`)".
- Category: error. Label: 🟢 FACT (wire + spec). Verified (M): `tshark -r CAP-019-btsnoop_hci.log -Y 'btrfcomm.len>0 && btrfcomm.dlci==4 &&
  frame.number>=780 && frame.number<=810' -T fields -e frame.number -e frame.p2p_dir -e data.data` (exit 0) → 804 (phone → Buds) `07 41 00 16 69 6e 2d 75 73
  65 …` (`696e2d757365` = "in-use"), 806 `07 42 00 11 00 64 …`, 810 the Buds' ACK `ff 01 00 08 07 41 69 6e 2d 75 73 65`.
- External evidence: developers.google.com/nearby/fast-pair/specifications/extensions/sass (fetched 2026-10-15): 0x41 "Indicate in use account key",
  Seeker → Provider, UTF-8 "in-use" at octets 4–9 + nonce + MAC — the string appears only in this message; 0x42 "Send custom data", one custom byte + nonce + MAC.
- Recommendation: correct by a dated note (rule 9a). Severity: minor.

**A93-PROT-04 — The open §6 item on SASS Codes `0x11`/`0x21`/`0x40`/`0x42`/`0x34` is answerable from the official page.**
- Location: `PROTOCOL.md:2696–2699` ("what do Fast Pair SASS … Codes `0x11`/`0x21`/`0x40`/`0x42` encode beyond their raw bytes? Is Code `0x34` … a
  periodic/keepalive SASS code …?").
- Category: undiscovered/closable finding. Label: 🟢 for the names and layouts (spec + matching lengths on the wire); 🟡 for any claim beyond the spec.
- Verified (M): the SASS page (fetched 2026-10-15) defines 0x10/0x11 capability (Provider length 4: version `0x0102` + 2 flag bytes — `CAP-019` 799 `07 11 00 04 01
  02 98 00`; Seeker length 20 with nonce + MAC — 786), 0x21 Get switching preference (length 0; answer 0x22), 0x34 Notify connection status (Provider → Seeker,
  sent on a connection-status change and as the answer to 0x33; octet 4 active flag 0/1/2, then encrypted status and nonce, no MAC), 0x40 Notify Audio switch
  initiated connection, 0x41 in-use account key, 0x42 custom data.
- Recommendation: record the names and layouts as a dated Update (🟢 for the spec's definitions and the matching lengths); keep 🔴 only for the encrypted
  contents; drop "periodic/keepalive" (the spec says 0x34 is event-driven). This is out of the app's scope (ADR-008 leaves SASS out), so no code follows.
  Severity: minor.

  HYPOTHESIS (confirmed as used by the Buds Pro 2 specifically)") vs `PROTOCOL.md:1170` (2026-10-06, 🟢 FACT: no Battery Notification field in clear in
  `CAP-054`'s case-open advertisements) and `README.md:195` ("never matched on the wire").

- Recommendation: "🟢 mechanism (spec); 🔴 not observed from the Pixel Buds Pro 2 (`CAP-011`/`043`/`054`)". Severity: minor.

**A93-PROT-07 — The ATT `0x0044` notification burst item can be closed.**
- Location: `PROTOCOL.md:2777–2794` ("Every occurrence examined so far is another device's traffic … The item stays listed only because no check was run on
  every capture.").
- Category: closable open question. Label: 🟢 FACT (the scan below).
- Verified (M): over all 103 `btsnoop`/`btsnooz` logs (scratch script att44.sh: `btatt.opcode==0x1b && btatt.handle==0x0044` per connection handle, and
  `btatt.uuid16==0x180d` per handle) — notifications in 76 logs; every carrying handle shows the Heart Rate service in the same log, except in `CAP-060`…`066`
  and `CAP-072` `hci1.log.last`, where the carrier `0x0040`/`0x0041` is the LE peer `c8:cc:a8:e7:48:93` (LE Connection Complete in all 12 logs), which exposes
  Heart Rate in `CAP-072-btsnoop_hci1.log`. In `CAP-059` the Buds' LE address held `0x0041` earlier (frame 538), but the notifications (5529–5702) come after the
  handle's reuse by another peer (5237) with Heart Rate at 5305. The Buds' attribute table has no Heart Rate service (`CAP-034-FINDINGS.md` §4).
- Recommendation: tick the item with this command and result (positive control: `CAP-016` handle `0x0002`, 73 notifications, Heart Rate frame 760).
  Severity: minor.

**A93-PROT-08 — One frame number off by one.**

- Category: error (citation). Label: 🟢. Verified (M): `tshark -r CAP-059-btsnoop_hci.log -Y 'frame contains 03:0a:00:08:4c:b5:93:ed' -T fields -e
  frame.number` → 1508. Method note: a script extracted every backticked hex string of ≥ 5 bytes that has a `CAP-NNN` and a frame number in the preceding 220
  characters (38 in `PROTOCOL.md`, 9 in `DECISIONS.md`/`ARCHITECTURE.md`) and checked each with `frame.number==N && frame contains <bytes>` over every log of the
  capture: 36 matched (directly or, for 10 extraction artefacts, at the adjacent frame the sentence names), 1 real error (this one).
- Recommendation: 1509 → 1508. Severity: nit.

### Phase 3 — Captures (Capture Index, registry, folders; three read-only capture sub-reviews; CAPTURE/TESTPLAN/APP_TESTPLAN read in full)

- Location: `APP_TESTPLAN.md:27` ("thirteen MAESTRO requests at Connect"), `:371`, `:384`, `:407` (V16/W1/X2 "the thirteen requests"), `:37` ("fifteen MAESTRO requests" for
  1.3.0, contradicted by its own Y2 row `:431` "seventeen requests per full session, not fifteen"); `CAPTURE_BLUETOOTH_HCI_SNOOP.md:1783` (Group BH "thirteen MAESTRO
  requests (the twelve settings reads, `SubscribeRuntimeInfo`, then one `GetHardwareInfo`)" — itself 14), `:1800`, `:1812`, the `CAP-072`/`CAP-073` Index rows
  (`:2291–2292`); `CHANGELOG.md:143` (`[1.2.0]` "thirteen requests … instead of twelve settings reads plus the EQ read and the subscription").
- Evidence: `CAP-076-FINDINGS.md` §2 counts the 1.2.2 session S0 as "15 (13 reads, SRI, GHI)" and every 1.3.0 session as 17; the test asserts `5 +
  settingReads.size` = 17 for 1.3.0 (`BudsRepositoryImplTest.kt:467–471`); "thirteen" is the number of `ReadSetting` requests only (the EQ's `4:16` + 12 settings).

**A93-CAP-05 — `PROTOCOL.md` and `CAP-058` name the link-key type wrongly: P-192, not P-256.** (S, re-verified M)
- Location: `PROTOCOL.md:2188–2189` ("the link key is type **0x05** (authenticated P-256) … type **0x04** (unauthenticated P-256)"); `CAP-058-FINDINGS.md:89/93/96`
  and its EVENT-NOTES key-type cells. `CAP-067-FINDINGS.md:233` has it right.
- Evidence: `tshark -r CAP-058-btsnoop_hci.log -Y 'frame.number==2235' -V | grep -i 'key type'` → "Key Type: Authenticated Combination Key, P-192 (0x05)".
  External: Bluetooth Core Specification, HCI Link Key Notification event — key types 0x04/0x05 are the P-192 combination keys, 0x07/0x08 the P-256 ones (the
  Wireshark dissector prints the spec's names; the spec itself not fetched this session).
- Category: error in a recorded protocol fact (a statement about the pairing's security level). Label: 🟢. Recommendation: a dated correction note (maintainer
  approval, `AGENTS.md` §6) in `PROTOCOL.md` and `CAP-058-FINDINGS.md`. Severity: **major**.

### Phase 4 — Architecture (`ARCHITECTURE.md` read in full; every §3.2 constant and named class checked against the code)

**A93-ARCH-01 — §3.1's ANC reconciliation row describes 1.2.x behaviour as current.**
- Location: `ARCHITECTURE.md:329` (§3.1 table, ANC row) — "every `ancMode` carries its cause … **reset at Connect** … **Since the 1.2.0 rebuild (ADR-061):** the claim
  is held while the noise-control tab is on screen, so such a `Notify` (a press-and-hold on a bud) reaches the app there". Since 1.3.0 the cause is **not** reset
  at Connect (ADR-065 Q4, `ai-sessions/0091` §B) and there is no hold (ADR-065); the mode also comes from the settings stream, which the row never mentions —
  the 1.3.0 rule sits in a separate §3.1 bullet (lines 286–291) that the table does not point to.
- Category: stale / internal inconsistency in the document the code is built from. Label: 🟢. Verified (M): `BudsRepositoryImpl.kt` (`causeOfStream`,
  `causeOfReadAnswer`; no `setAncTabShown`/`HOLD_RECLAIM_*` left — `grep -rn` → 0 in `android/*/src`).
- Recommendation: rewrite the ANC row in place (`ARCHITECTURE.md` is not under rule 9a's keep-history scope) with the 1.3.0 sources (Notify on a claim, the
  settings stream, the `4:13` read) and the cause rules as built; move the 1.2.x hold into §6.0b's history paragraph. Severity: **major**.

  services', ADR-062 Update 2 and ADR-063") vs `ARCHITECTURE.md:207` ("gear → Settings is Dark mode · Case sounds · Use different Buds"); `ARCHITECTURE.md:922–923`
  (§9 "Local state persistence: … since 1.2.1 … the 'Noise-control channel' choice are stored") vs `DarkModeSettingsStore.kt:34` ("the key `hold_reclaim_mode` … is
  ignored since `ai-sessions/0091`").

**A93-ARCH-03 — `BluetoothGatt` is named as owned by the hardware layer; no GATT client exists.**
- Location: `ARCHITECTURE.md:128` ("Owns raw `BluetoothSocket`/`BluetoothGatt` objects"), `:247` (`BudsTransport` "where applicable, `BluetoothGatt` (secondary —
  case/charging characteristics)"), `:24`; vs `ARCHITECTURE.md` §2 line 91 / §13 (no GATT client) and `grep -rn BluetoothGatt android --include=*.kt` → one KDoc

### Phase 8 — Application (`android/`; two read-only sub-reviews — `:data`, and `:hardware`/`:domain`/`:app`/`:ui`/CI — every item below re-checked in the code by the main session; marks: M = found by the main session, S = found by a sub-review and re-verified here)

Paths: `data/…` = `android/data/src/main/kotlin/io/github/tedsluis/opencontrolpixelbuds/data/`, likewise `hardware/…`, `app/…`, `ui/…`.

**A93-APP-01 — "Use different Buds" acts on a second `BudsCompanionPairing`; the repository keeps connecting to the old Buds by name.** (S)
- Location: `app/…/MainActivity.kt:143` `private val companionPairing by lazy { BudsCompanionPairing(this) }` and `:273` `companionPairing.forgetAssociations()`; the
  repository uses the `@Singleton` from `app/…/di/RepositoryModule.kt:72–73` (`bondedDeviceProvider = companionPairing::bondedDevice`, `:86`);
  `hardware/…/BudsCompanionPairing.kt:67/97/120` — `chooseAgainRequested` is an instance field that turns the name fallback off.
- Problem: the flag is set on the Activity's instance only; the singleton's `bondedDevice()` still falls back to "the single bonded Pixel Buds" by name, so a
  Connect or pull right after "Use different Buds" reaches the old Buds — contrary to `ARCHITECTURE.md` §9 ("until a new association exists no device is taken by its
  name"); a rotation also creates a fresh Activity instance with the flag false.
- Category: application defect. Label: 🟢 (code reading; not observed on hardware — `CAP-068` tested "Use different Buds" with one pair only). Recommendation: inject
  the singleton into `MainActivity` (the Activity still launches the `IntentSender`), or keep the flag in one shared holder; a JVM test with two lookups. Severity:
  **major** (a user-facing function does not do what it says; can talk to the wrong device).

**A93-APP-02 — A re-open's "Changed since the app last saw it" is overwritten by the equal `4:13` answer (CAP-076 defect (a)).** (S, confirmed in code)
- Location: `data/…/BudsRepositoryImpl.kt:385–386` `if (_ancMode.value != null && mode != _ancMode.value) AncModeCause.CHANGED_SINCE_LAST_READ else
  AncModeCause.READ` — used for both the snapshot `Get`'s Notify and the `ReadSetting 4:13` answer; the second, equal answer returns `READ` and replaces the cause.
- Evidence: `CAP-076-FINDINGS.md` §6.2 (12 of 12 sessions: the Notify first, then the read); the test "the automatic re-open subscribes … again"
  (`BudsRepositoryImplTest.kt` ≈ 3244–3280) answers the `Get` and the read with **different** modes, which never happens on hardware, so the defect passes it.
- Recommendation (`CAP-076` P1): return `null` (keep the cause) for an equal read, as `causeOfStream` does; fix the test to answer both with the same mode.
  Severity: minor (an (i) line).

**A93-APP-03 — A tap's `Set` can take the `Get`'s own Notify as its answer (CAP-076 defect (b), the silent tile tap).** (S, code path confirmed; 🟡 as the cause)
- Location: `data/…/BudsRepositoryImpl.kt:695–707` — every Notify is emitted to `_ancNotifies` (the `Get`'s wait), then to `_ancOutcomes` (the `Set`'s wait) even
  when `answersGet`; `:973–993` — `ancSetOnClaim` subscribes to `_ancOutcomes` (`sendAndAwaitOnClaim`, `CoroutineStart.UNDISPATCHED`, `:1693`) and returns
  `Success` on `AncOutcome.Notified`. The repository scope is `SupervisorJob() + Dispatchers.Default` (`app/…/di/RepositoryModule.kt:57`): the `Get` waiter can
  resume on another thread, send the `Set` and subscribe before the inbound thread emits `Notified` for the `Get`'s answer.
- Evidence: `CAP-076-FINDINGS.md` §5.3 (06:46:08.96: no `Set` on the wire, no failure line, no toast). Recommendation (`CAP-076` P2): do not emit
  `AncOutcome.Notified` for a Notify that answers a `Get`; a unit test for that rule (the interleaving itself cannot happen under `StandardTestDispatcher`).
  Severity: minor.

**A93-APP-04 — Settings-stream pushes can end a pending read's wait.** (S)
- Location: `data/…/BudsRepositoryImpl.kt:770–773` — `RoutedFrame.Setting` (RESPONSE **and** `SERVER_STREAM`) → `_maestroReplies.tryEmit(MaestroReply.Setting(value))`
  without the `stream` flag; the reads' `accept` predicates match any `Setting` of the field (≈ `:1555`, `:1625`); the same for EQ (`MaestroReply.Value`).
- Problem: a push of the field being read (e.g. a `4:{13:n}` right after the subscription) completes the read as if it were the answer; the RESPONSE then
  arrives with nothing waiting. Harmless while both carry the same value; wrong if they differ.
- Recommendation: carry `stream` in the reply and accept only RESPONSEs; likewise `_ancModeFresh` (`applyAncState`) should not end a Message Stream `Get`'s wait
  (`refreshAncMode` waits on it — a stream packet can satisfy the Refresh and leave the real Notify classed as unsolicited). Severity: minor.

**A93-APP-05 — Narrow error-model gaps (`AGENTS.md` §3/§8).** (S)
- Location: `hardware/…/BudsCompanionPairing.kt:177` `manager.associate(…)` without `try/catch` (a `RuntimeException` leaves `associationInFlight = true`, every later
  tap reports "already in progress"); `hardware/…/RfcommBudsTransport.kt:336–341` — "still the current, open connection?" and `connection.auxSockets[channelId] = …`
  are not atomic: a loss in between leaves a DLCI 0x04 socket open after `closeAll()` (the one-connection-per-channel collision of `ai-sessions/0039`); the
  application scope has no `CoroutineExceptionHandler`, and `DebugSettingsStore`/`DarkModeSettingsStore` flows have no `.catch` for a corrupt preferences file.
- Recommendation: catch and reset; do the check-and-put under the transport's lock and re-check after; a scope handler that logs; `.catch { emit(emptyPreferences()) }`.
  Severity: minor.

**A93-APP-06 — Tests that do not test what they say.** (S, verified)
- `android/data/src/test/…/codec/SettingsStreamFixtures.kt:119` `SET_TRANSPARENT_CAP051_1527 = "0812001401e8e880" + 16 × "00"` — the real frame is
  `0812001401e8e880e4e68882be02fffe63abde07ef49dae8` (`tshark -r CAP-051-btsnoop_hci.log -Y 'frame.number==1527' -T fields -e data.data`): not real bytes (`AGENTS.md`
  §11), and unused. The fuzz tests (`SettingsStreamCodecTest` "fuzzed stream packets never throw", `OversizedLengthTest`, `CodecRouterTest` "random chunks never throw")
  run through `CodecRouter.decodeGuarded`, which converts a thrown exception into `onMalformed`; none passes `onDecoderFault` (`grep -rn onDecoderFault
  android/data/src/test` → 0), so a decoder that throws still passes the "never throw" test. `BudsRepositoryImplTest` ≈ 3097–3170 still names "ADR-062 Update 2 … a
  re-claim's Get answer" / "Notify on the held claim".
- Recommendation: real bytes or delete; count `onDecoderFault` calls and assert 0; rename the stale tests. Severity: minor.

**A93-APP-07 — Accessibility.** (S)
- `ui/…/SettingsUi.kt:151–164`, `ui/…/ControlsScreen.kt:212–219`, `ui/…/DebugScreen.kt:61–64`: the label is a separate `Text`, so TalkBack reads "Switch, on" with no
  name; `ui/…/EqScreen.kt:356–365`: the five band sliders have no `contentDescription`/`stateDescription` (the balance slider has one, `:292`);
  `ui/…/OpenControlNavHost.kt:322`: the tab icon repeats the visible label.
- Recommendation: `Modifier.toggleable(…, role = Role.Switch)` on the row with `onCheckedChange = null`; semantics on each slider; `contentDescription = null` on
  the tab icon. Severity: minor. External evidence: developer.android.com, "Make apps more accessible" (merge a control with its label) — not fetched this session.

**A93-APP-08 — UI wording and small code issues.** (S unless marked)
- The mode has four labels: "ANC mode: ACTIVE" (`ui/…/AncScreen.kt:160–162`), "Noise cancellation" (the buttons), "Active" (the tile, `domain/…/AncTile.kt:42`),
  "Connected — ANC: ACTIVE" (`app/…/OpenControlApplication.kt:96`); enum and class names on screen (`AncScreen.kt:186` `connectionState::class.simpleName`,
  `EqScreen.kt:207` "HEAVY BASS"); "Use different Buds" removes the association without a confirmation (`ui/…/SettingsMenu.kt:201`); lint `ModifierParameter` at
  `ui/…/SettingsMenu.kt:96` (M: confirmed in `ui/build/reports/lint-results-debug.xml`); KDoc of removed 1.2.x parameters (`SettingsMenu.kt:78–80`, `:107–108`) and the
  `MESSAGE_STREAM_LINGER_MS` KDoc ("also after the noise-control tab … is left (ADR-061)"); the log line of a same-mode push prints the kept cause
  (`data/…/BudsRepositoryImpl.kt:419`, `CAP-076` defect (c)); `MainActivity.kt:217` a `Toast` on the Activity from `applicationScope`; `BudsForegroundService.kt:224`
  `getLaunchIntentForPackage()` may be null; stale `TODO(verify)` in `BudsCompanionPairing.kt:51–53, 144–145` (hardware-confirmed since `0041`/`0043`).
- Recommendation: one label function from `ANC_MODE_LIST_ORDER`; human words for states and presets; a confirm dialog; the trivial fixes. Severity: nit (each).

**A93-APP-09 — `.github/workflows/lint-docs.yml` runs with the default token permissions.** (S, verified: no `permissions:` key)
- Location: `.github/workflows/lint-docs.yml`. `.github/workflows/android.yml` sets `contents: read` and pins actions by SHA. Recommendation: `permissions: contents: read`. External
  evidence: GitHub Docs, "Automatic token authentication — Modifying the permissions for the GITHUB_TOKEN" (least privilege) — not fetched this session. Severity: minor.

**Checked and not a finding (Phase 8):** `AGENTS.md` §1/§2/§3/§7/§12 hold (sub-review, spot-checked M: manifests, AGPL header grep, `START_NOT_STICKY`, CDM pattern,
ring buffer, `FLAG_IMMUTABLE`, every receiver unregistered in `awaitClose`, MAC redaction in `BleLogger.describe`); the §3.2 constants (Phase 4); the 1.3.0 removal is
complete in the code; field 18 not applied; two `TODO(verify)` in `:data` (`EqFrame.kt:39`, `CaseBatteryFrame.kt:53`) are still valid open hypotheses. Rejected from the
sub-reviews: "`AGENTS.md` §2 still lists `BLUETOOTH_SCAN`" — §2 lists what *may* be requested (with `neverForLocation`); the app requesting less is compliant.
`targetSdk = 34` (lint `OldTargetApi`) is a decision (ADR-029), listed in §7.

### Phase 9 — APK reverse engineering (`v1.0.955078536-10253511` only — Checkpoint 0: the newer APK is not decompiled; `REVERSE_ENGINEERING.md` read in full)

Mechanical work in this phase (ADR-017 boundary — search, list, explain; no relevance decision, no promotion): the single-writer claim of `0090`
re-checked (`grep -rn 'Lgea;->I(Ljava/lang/String;Lgcq;)V\|Lgck;->I(…)V' apktool-output/smali*/` → 1 site, `fxb.smali:1237`, ✔); the field-23 path traced
end to end; a "Revision 6" search; the users of the marker types `qhq`/`qjf`/`qis`/`qiq` (settings 23/31/37/35) listed (`grep -rlw` → only `qhr.java` and their own
files, plus `qhq` in `fxb`/`gea`/`gck`). Findings on the document follow; the new leads are in §5b.

**A93-RE-01 — Self-references by line number have drifted.**
- Location: `REVERSE_ENGINEERING.md:988` "(line 894 above)" (the bullet is now at ≈ 946), `:1234` "field register (line 856)" (now ≈ 1021–1029), `:1247` "line 999
  above", `:1433` "bonus register (line ~1068)" (now ≈ 1150–1176).
- Category: stale cross-references (the file grew ~3,000 lines since). Label: 🟢. Verified (M). Recommendation: refer by entry and heading ("the `qhr` register
  above"), never by line. Severity: minor.

**A93-RE-02 — A broken edit and a duplicated discovery in the `frb`/`fuh`/`glk`/`gjv` entry.**
- Location: `REVERSE_ENGINEERING.md:3374` starts mid-sentence ("decoded shape) is now the more promising route — pursued this same session …") — the start of the
  sentence is missing; the `ai-sessions/0013` Update (dated 2026-09-13) sits after the `0023` Update (2026-09-15), and both report finding `gjv.p()`'s caller
  (`ftw.smali:337`) "via a different strategy than the prior (failed) passes" — `0023`'s "missed by both prior passes" is wrong, `0013` had found it two days earlier;
  `:3284` "6 classes implement it" lists seven (`fxm`, `fvp`, `fwc`, `fwx`, `fxg`, `fyq`, `geg`).
- Category: corruption / chronology / arithmetic. Label: 🟢. Verified (M). Recommendation: restore the sentence from git history (`git log -S 'decoded shape) is now
  the more promising' -- REVERSE_ENGINEERING.md`), add a correction note on `0023`'s claim, "seven classes". Severity: minor.

**A93-RE-03 — The "Correlation status with `PROTOCOL.md`" table stops on 2026-10-06.**
- Location: `REVERSE_ENGINEERING.md:4541–4580` (last row `ai-sessions/0073`, then an empty template row). Missing: field 21 = "Volume level notifications" (🟢,
  2026-10-06, `0072`, `PROTOCOL.md` §4.5.8a); `GetHardwareInfo` field 7 read by position (🟢, 2026-10-09, `0082`/ADR-058); the wall-clock field 2 (🟡 recorded,
  2026-10-07, `0077`); setting 13 = the app's only ANC-state source (🟢 §4.1, 2026-10-14, `0090`).
- Category: incompleteness (the table exists so a finding is not "rediscovered"). Label: 🟢. Verified (M). Recommendation: add the four rows; drop the empty row.
  Severity: minor.

**A93-RE-04 — The call-graph block still shows four `GetSoftwareInfo` calls.**
- Location: `REVERSE_ENGINEERING.md:4512` — "`fxm.i()` → `fxm.c(goq, goq)` x4 [`maestro_pw.Maestro/GetSoftwareInfo` pw_rpc unary …]". `0073` established (the `qjb`
  entry's Update of 2026-10-06, `fxm.java:53`, `npy.java:82–90`, `fxk.java:22–36`) that the app opens pending unary calls and sends **no** `GetSoftwareInfo` request —
  the Buds' announcement completes them (0 requests in 80 logs; `0090` E-17: 0 in 96).
- Category: stale. Label: 🟢. Verified (M). Recommendation: "opens 4 pending unary calls (no request on the wire); the Buds' unsolicited announcement completes
  them". Severity: minor.

**A93-RE-05 — The UUID register keeps a superseded 🟡.**
- Location: `REVERSE_ENGINEERING.md:4077` (row `25e97ff7-…` "pigweed internal rfcomm socket": "🟡 HYPOTHESIS (strong) that Sent-direction payload content specifically
  carries `libmaestro`'s settings commands") — 🟢 since ADR-019 (2026-08-30) and ADR-034 (pw_rpc `maestro_pw.Maestro`/`WriteSetting`, 2026-09-20).
- Category: stale label. Label: 🟢. Verified (M). Recommendation: "🟢 (ADR-019, ADR-034)". Severity: minor.

**A93-RE-06 — Smaller document defects.**
- `REVERSE_ENGINEERING.md:2506–2508` — an `ai-sessions/0069` pointer inserted inside a sentence ("Included here only to keep this *(pointer …)* register complete");
  `:4144` "If the Message Stream framing hypothesis (`PROTOCOL.md` §2.1) is confirmed" (🟢 since ADR-009); `:4485` the native-libraries note still offers
  "`pbtk-from-binary`" (ADR-041: `pbtk` does not apply; same in `APK_REVERSE_ENGINEERING_PROCEDURE.md`'s intro, "running `pbtk`"); `:4595` "treat static findings as
  🟡 HYPOTHESIS until correlated" while the document labels code existence 🟢 (code) — say "a protocol reading from code is 🟡 until a capture shows it"; the `qhr`
  register rows 5 and 14 (`:1155`, `:1164`) still read "not independently named" / "write-only" with no pointer to the `0073` names (5 = diagnostics, 14 = eartip
  seal test, `:1449–1451`); the `MaestroDeviceSettingsProviderService` entry's "Open questions" still lists field 5's and field 6's caller (answered by `0073`/`0025`).
- Category: stale / wording. Label: 🟢. Verified (M). Recommendation: fix each in place or by a one-line pointer. Severity: nit.

**Checked and not a finding (Phase 9, M):** field 23 — the read handler stores it and nothing reads it back (`fxb.java:355–357` → `gck.H` → `gcg` case 1 writes the
raw bytes into `gct.c` → `gdw.y`; `grep -rn 'Lgdw;->y:Lgct;' apktool-output/smali*/` → one `iput` in `gcg.smali`, no `iget`) — so the register's "case 23 → `H`" and
`0073`'s "stored as raw bytes and never read" agree; "Revision 6" (the DIS firmware-revision string) is not in the APK (`grep -rIl 'Revision 6\|FirmwareRevision'` over
sources and `res/` → 0; positive control "pigweed internal rfcomm" → 1 file) — it is GATT, served to Play services, consistent with ADR-025; the GSND literals stay
absent (as `0073`).

## 5. Undiscovered protocol leads

### 5a. From the captures

**L93-1 — `CAP-021`'s four GSND AUDIO waves are assistant sessions (the opener is on the wire).** 🟡 HYPOTHESIS (strong), 🟢 for the frames.
- Evidence (M): `tshark -r CAP-021-btsnoop_hci.log -Y 'btrfcomm.len>0 && (btrfcomm.dlci==8 || btrfcomm.dlci==9) && frame contains 01:09:00:03' -T fields -e
  frame.number -e frame.time -e frame.p2p_dir -e data.data` (exit 0): the Buds' GSND CONTROL `01 09 00 03 0a 01 03` at 2092 (08:02:29.503), 2963
  (08:02:50.108), 3645 (08:03:27.109) and 4340 (08:03:58.307) — the four wave starts of `DESKRESEARCH_FINDINGS.md` 2026-10-03 item 4 — each followed within
  1–2 ms by the first DLCI 0x0a frame (2093 at 08:02:29.504; 1123 DLCI 0x0a payload frames in all = the burst of `PROTOCOL.md` §6). Every wave also carries
  `01 09 00 03 0a 01 05`, `… 01 01` and `… 01 21` (e.g. 2297, 2340, 2341). `CAP-069` showed the same opener at 3 of 3 filmed assistant sessions
  (`PROTOCOL.md:3658`, 🟢 for that capture); `CAP-021`'s press-and-hold was set to the digital assistant during the capture (`CAP-021-FINDINGS.md` §3).
- Not yet recorded anywhere (`grep -rn '2092\|0a 01 03'` over `PROTOCOL.md`, `DESKRESEARCH_FINDINGS.md` and `CAP-021`'s files → 0; positive control
  `CAP-069-FINDINGS.md:93`).
- Proposal: a dated note on `PROTOCOL.md` §6's DLCI 0x0a item and `DESKRESEARCH_FINDINGS.md` 2026-10-03 item 4 — "the four waves begin with the assistant-session
  opener (🟡 assistant sessions started by press-and-holds)"; the codes `0a 01 05`/`01`/`21` as an open sub-question (session states?). Test: a filmed
  assistant hold with the app's GSND channels logged (already the shape of `CAP-069` section III).

**L93-2 — The SASS Group `0x07` codes are defined by the official page** — see A93-PROT-03/04 (spec fetched 2026-10-15; `CAP-019` 786–810).

**L93-3 — ATT `0x0044` is never a Buds link** — closed by scan, A93-PROT-07.

### 5b. From the APK

**L93-4 — Setting 23's content is unknown to this app version: the Buds send a field the schema does not have.** 🟢 for the code and wire facts, 🔴 for the meaning.
- Evidence (M): `qhq` (the setting-23 type) decodes to **0 fields** (`python3 -I scripts/decode_rawmessageinfo.py jadx-output/sources/defpackage/qhq.java` → "0
  field(s)"); the Buds answer `23:{4:{1:0}}` in every log (`DESKRESEARCH_FINDINGS.md` 2026-10-06 item 3, "constant in every log"); the app keeps the unknown bytes
  (`gcg` case 1 → `gct.c` → `gdw.y`) and never reads them (one `iput`, no `iget`). The field is therefore defined by firmware newer than this app's schema, or by
  another product family.
- Proposal: when the maintainer decides to decompile `v1.0.990706425-10260911` (`TODO.md` §4), check `qhq`'s successor for a field 4 and the reader of setting
  **39** (asked only by that version, `CAP-069` 1333/7261/8465 → `UNKNOWN`) — both with `schema_batch_extractor` and `citation_checker`, as `APK_REVERSE_ENGINEERING_PROCEDURE.md` §7 says.

**L93-5 — Settings 24, 26, 30, 31, 32 stay unnameable from this version** (no write site, no reader case in `fxb`; 31 = the 0-field marker `qjf`, used only by
`qhr.java`): a checked negative of this pass, recorded so the next pass starts from the newer APK rather than this one.

- S90-c1 (major candidate) PROTOCOL §4.1 0090 FACT bullet: "receives a SERVER_STREAM 4:{13:n} for every mode change the Buds report … 73 of 73 Buds-side changes" — the 73 is the class *defined* as "Notify with a stream packet within 1 s" (DESKRESEARCH 2026-10-14 §2 table), so 73/73 is circular; the same entry's next bullet says 12 Buds-side changes were NOT pushed ("12 of 77"). Buds-side Notify events in the table: 73 + 2(pairs) + 12 + 1 + 1 = 89 (or 91 counting the pairs' Notifies). "every mode change" contradicts "Not pushed … 12". And "12 of 77": 77 is not any sum in the table (73+2+1+1 = 77 excludes the 12 themselves, so the ratio should be 12 of 89). Propagated: 0090 Summary, PROTOCOL §8 row, CHANGELOG 1.3.0 Known issues ("12 of 77"), README? Re-derive with --census in Phase 2.

WORKSTATION's npm recipe takes ownership of /usr/local instead. Also a contrast for WS-c1: a vendor dnf repo with gpgcheck=1 is the documented norm.
- EXT-2 (PT-c48 lead) developers.google.com/nearby/fast-pair/specifications/extensions/sass (fetched 2026-10-15): Group 0x07 "Audio switch": 0x10 Get capability, 0x11 Notify capability (both directions; version 0x0102, Provider length 4, Seeker 20 with nonce+MAC), 0x12 Set multipoint state, 0x20 Set switching preference, 0x21 Get switching preference (Seeker→Provider, length 0; answer 0x22), 0x22 Notify switching preference, 0x30–0x33, 0x34 Notify connection status (Provider→Seeker, on connection change on multipoint Providers and as answer to 0x33; octet 4 active flag 0/1/2, then encrypted status, nonce, no MAC), 0x40 Notify Audio switch initiated connection, 0x41 Indicate in use account key (Seeker→Provider, UTF-8 "in-use" octets 4–9 + nonce + MAC — "in-use" appears ONLY in 0x41), 0x42 Send custom data (Seeker→Provider, 1 custom byte + nonce + MAC; Provider then updates its advertisement), 0x43 Set drop connection target. ⇒ PROTOCOL §6 item (line ~2696, "what do 0x11/0x21/0x40/0x42 encode … is 0x34 periodic/keepalive") is answerable by spec at the naming level; and PROTOCOL §4.5.2 (line ~1706) "Codes 0x11/0x21/0x34/0x40/0x41/0x42, the last containing the ASCII string in-use" contradicts the spec (in-use is in 0x41) — verify on CAP-019 bytes (Phase 2).
- HK-c2 android/domain/bin/ (25 files: IDE build output incl. copies of Kotlin test sources, e.g. domain/bin/test/…/AncTileTest.kt) is not in any .gitignore; it appears as ?? in every session's git status since ≥0083 and pollutes greps over android/. Add bin/ to .gitignore (or android/.gitignore). minor.
- PT-c48 VERIFIED (M): CAP-019 804 phone→Buds 07 41 00 16 696e2d757365 ('in-use') … = spec 0x41 'Indicate in use account key'; 806 07 42 00 11 00 64 … = 0x42 'Send custom data' (custom byte 0x00 then nonce/MAC per spec layout? len 0x11=17 ✓); 799 Buds 07 11 00 04 01 02 98 00 = Provider 'Notify capability' version 0x0102 flags 0x9800 (len 4 ✓); 786 phone 07 11 00 14 … len 20 ✓ (Seeker with nonce+MAC); 805 07 40 00 11 …. PROTOCOL §4.5.2 (line ~1706) 'the last [0x42] containing the ASCII string in-use' is wrong — it is 0x41 (frame 804). Command: tshark -r CAP-019-btsnoop_hci.log -Y 'btrfcomm.len>0 && btrfcomm.dlci==4 && frame.number>=780 && frame.number<=810' -T fields -e frame.number -e frame.p2p_dir -e data.data (exit 0).
- LEAD-A (new, M): CAP-021's four GSND AUDIO (DLCI 0x0a) waves each start with the Buds' GSND CONTROL 01 09 00 03 0a 01 03 1–2 ms before the first audio frame: 2092 08:02:29.503 → 0x0a 2093 29.504; 2963 08:02:50.108; 3645 08:03:27.109; 4340 08:03:58.307 (= the four wave starts of DESKRESEARCH 2026-10-03 item 4) — the same opener CAP-069 §3 found 3/3 for filmed assistant sessions (PROTOCOL.md:3658). Not recorded in PROTOCOL/DESKRESEARCH/CAP-021. ⇒ the 🔴 'trigger not on film' narrows to 🟡 strong: assistant sessions (press-and-hold set to assistant in that capture). Also 0a 01 05 / 0a 01 01 / 0a 01 21 follow in each wave (2297–2341 …): candidate session-state codes. Command: tshark -r CAP-021-btsnoop_hci.log -Y 'btrfcomm.len>0 && (btrfcomm.dlci==8||btrfcomm.dlci==9) && frame contains 01:09:00:03' -T fields -e frame.number -e frame.time -e frame.p2p_dir -e data.data (exit 0); DLCI 10 frames: 1123 (positive control = the burst count in PROTOCOL §6).
- DEC-c7/S91-c3 checked: a field-18 EQ frame (stream or read) is not applied (BudsRepositoryImpl.kt:735–741, ) — consistent with ADR-065 item 2. Not a finding.
- PT-c49 VERIFIED/closable (M): ATT notification on handle 0x0044 (`btatt.opcode==0x1b && btatt.handle==0x0044`) occurs in 76 of 103 logs (scratch att44.txt; script att44.sh); in every one, every carrying connection handle also shows the Heart Rate service (`btatt.uuid16==0x180d`) in the same log, EXCEPT CAP-060…066 and CAP-072 hci1.log.last, where the carrier (0x0040/0x0041) is the LE peer c8:cc:a8:e7:48:93 (LE Connection Complete, all 12 such logs) — the same peer exposes Heart Rate in CAP-072-btsnoop_hci1.log (0x0040/0x0041). In CAP-059 the Buds' own LE address (04:00:6e:cf:6e:07, frame 538) held handle 0x0041 earlier, but the 0x0044 notifications (5529–5702) follow the handle's re-use by 54:b7:53:5f:61:0e (5237) whose HR service is at 5305. ⇒ no 0x0044 notification on a Buds link in any capture; PROTOCOL §6 item (line ~2777–2794, "stays listed only because no check was run on every capture") can be closed (proposal; maintainer). Note: CAP-064's 0x0044 values carry what looks like an IPv4 header (0x45 …) — another device's data, out of scope.
- PT-c7 DONE (M): scripted check of every PROTOCOL.md backticked hex string (≥5 bytes) with a CAP-NNN and a frame number in the 220 characters before it (scratch cites2.py/checkcites.py; `tshark -r <log> -Y 'frame.number==N && frame contains <first 32 bytes>'` over every log of that capture): 38 extracted, 31 OK at once; of the 7 mismatches, 6 are extraction artefacts (the hex belongs to the next frame named in the sentence — verified: CAP-069 3166, CAP-064 9927, CAP-068 A2036 (hci2.log.last) all contain the cited bytes; 1583 and 3659 are negative/frame-less phrases); ONE real error: PROTOCOL.md:111–112 cites CAP-059 "frame 1509 `03 0a 00 08 4c b5 93 ed 88 3f 5a ef`" — the bytes are in frame 1508 (`tshark -r CAP-059-btsnoop_hci.log -Y 'frame contains 03:0a:00:08:4c:b5:93:ed'` → 1508). nit (off-by-one).
- DECISIONS+ARCHITECTURE citation check: 9 extracted, 5 OK, 4 extraction artefacts (an md5, a list of bytes, a later frame) — no real error.
- S91-c2 refined: BudsRepositoryImplTest.kt:467–471 asserts `5 + settingReads.size` = 17 requests; the 0091 RESULT §C "the Connect-sequence counts (15 requests)" is wrong (nit).
- S91-c5: 0091 PROMPT Phase F item 10 asked ARCHITECTURE §2.4/§3.1/§6.0b to turn the hold/re-claim into history with a pointer; RESULT §F claims §2.4 "(the 1.2.x card as history)" and §3.1 "(the stream rule)" done — but §2.4's tree (line 153) and §3.1's ANC row (line 329) still state the 1.2.x behaviour as current → origin of A93-ARCH-01/02.

The same P-256 wording is in PROTOCOL.md:2188-2189 (needs maintainer approval to change). Severity raised to major (wrong in PROTOCOL.md).

#### CAP-075 (read in full; appendix skimmed)
#### Check 6(b) — "8 of 19" vs "8 of 31"
Both are correct, with different denominators, per CAP-075-FINDINGS.md §4 :166-171: 31 = every hold in set 2's film (8 within a second + 2 late + 3 overdrawn + 2 after a user action + 16 never = 31); 19 = the holds made during the three "Take it back" chains (8 + 2 + 3 + 6 = 19); "Show a hint" 9 holds (0 within a second, 1 after a user action, 8 never); the stopped period 3 (0). All 8 shown holds fall in the chains, so "8 of 31" (DECISIONS.md:2782 ADR-062 Update; CAP-076-FINDINGS.md:154/:326; CAP-075 FINDINGS:18/:294) is the whole-film figure and "8 of 19" (FINDINGS:257/:268/:327) the per-position figure. Only a wording risk: in the ADR-062 Update (DECISIONS.md:2782, copied in FINDINGS:294) "8 of 31 holds shown" stands in the sentence about mode 2, where the mode-2 figure is 8 of 19 → suggest "8 of the film's 31 holds (8 of the 19 made during the chains, 0 of the 9 under the hint)".

### sub_data

#### sub_data — :data audit (1.3.0, b9828ba) — progressive findings

#### BudsRepositoryImpl.kt lines 1–1480 (in progress)

- D-01 major — BudsRepositoryImpl.kt:385-386 `causeOfReadAnswer`: `if (_ancMode.value != null && mode != _ancMode.value) CHANGED_SINCE_LAST_READ else READ`.
  Confirmed CAP-076 §6.2: snapshot Get's Notify (answersGet → causeOfReadAnswer → CHANGED_SINCE_LAST_READ, emitAncMode sets _ancMode = new mode) then
  ReadSetting 4:13 answer with same mode → mode == _ancMode → READ → emitAncMode overwrites cause. Fix (P1): return AncModeCause? and `null` when
  mode == _ancMode.value && _ancMode.value != null (emitAncMode then keeps cause); return type nullable; test with CAP-076 H1 4094 → 4135.
  Note: also affects Notify-answers-Get when the stream push already set the mode → READ overwrites CHANGED_BY_BUDS (same fix covers it).
- D-02 major — handleRoutedFrame Notify branch (~702-708): `_ancNotifies.tryEmit(...)` then `emitAncMode` then `_ancOutcomes.tryEmit(AncOutcome.Notified(it))`
  unconditionally, also when answersGet. setAncModeAfterGet → ancSetOnClaim → sendAndAwaitOnClaim(_ancOutcomes,…) (~973). On a multi-threaded dispatcher the
  Get waiter can resume and subscribe to _ancOutcomes before line ~708 → the Set takes the Get's Notify as Notified → Success (line ~993) without ACK.
  Fix: do not emit Notified for a Notify that answersGet; and/or stamp outcomes with a sequence (outcome seq > seq at Set write) and filter.
- D-03 nit — applyAncState line 419 logs `(cause ?: _ancModeCause.value)` → a same-mode push logs the kept cause (CAP-076 §4.4). Fix: log `cause?.name ?: "unchanged"`.
- D-04 minor — readSettings/readAncSetting accept (~1555, ~1625): `(it is MaestroReply.Setting && it.value.field == field)`; handleRoutedFrame emits
  MaestroReply.Setting also for stream pushes (frame.stream true). A stream push of the field being read (or a 4:{13:n} push between the subscribe and
  readAncSetting, sent back-to-back) is taken as the read's answer; the real RESPONSE then arrives with nothing waiting; an error/unreadable RpcResult for
  READ_SETTING of field N can then end field N+1's wait (accept matches any READ_SETTING Result). Fix: carry `stream` in MaestroReply.Setting and accept only
  non-stream answers for reads (`!it.stream`).
- D-05 nit — companion MESSAGE_STREAM_LINGER_MS KDoc: "also after the noise-control tab or the app is left (ADR-061)" — describes ADR-061's hold, removed
  by ADR-065 item 5. Fix: drop the clause.
- D-06 nit — GET_RESPONSE_TIMEOUT_MS KDoc "Wait for a fresh ANC Notify after a manual Refresh" — it also bounds every tap's Get (setAncModeAfterGet). Also
  `launchInitialEqRead` now runs six steps (EQ, settings, runtime info, settings stream, 4:13, hardware info) — name misleads.
- D-07 nit — refreshBattery: `resubscribeJob = scope.launch {…}` overwrites a still-running earlier job without cancelling it (only the last is cancelled by
  cancelClaimJobs). Fix: `resubscribeJob?.cancel()` first, or skip if active.
- D-08 nit — readAncSetting: `Maestro.readSettingRequest(...) ?: return@withLock` returns silently (no log) unlike every other failure in that function.
- D-09 minor — applyAncState → updateAncAvailability computes `_ancAvailabilityProvisional = clock() - messageStreamOpenedAt < 2 s`; for a settings-stream
  push / 4:13 answer (MAESTRO channel, not the claim) the ADR-024 "stale within 2 s of the Message Stream open" rule does not apply, yet a stream packet
  arriving within 2 s of a claim's open is flagged provisional. Fix: pass a `provisional` flag; false for MAESTRO-sourced readings.
- D-10 nit — handleRoutedFrame `is AncFrame.Set -> emitAncMode(anc.mode)` (inbound Set) sets the mode without a cause, so the old cause sticks to a new mode.
  Either drop (the Buds never send Set) or surface as UnidentifiedFrame/log.

#### BudsRepositoryImpl.kt fully read; codec/*, SafeModeGate, SessionDiagnostics, SessionReopener, settings/* read
- D-02 addendum: application scope = `CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + Dispatchers.Default)` (app/di/RepositoryModule.kt:57), tile scope Dispatchers.Main
  (AncTileService.kt:79) → inbound collector and the tap's Get waiter run on different threads — the race is real. JVM tests with one test dispatcher cannot
  reproduce it. Best fix: in handleRoutedFrame emit `AncOutcome.Notified` only when `!answersGet` (the Get's answer can never be the Set's).
- D-11 minor — application scope has no CoroutineExceptionHandler; handleRoutedFrame (outside decodeGuarded) and `debugModeEnabled.collect` run in it.
  DataStore `.data` (DebugSettingsStore:43, DarkModeSettingsStore:38) has no `.catch { IOException -> emit(emptyPreferences()) }`: a corrupt prefs file
  throws into BudsRepositoryImpl's init collector → uncaught on Default → process crash. setDebugModeEnabled/setDarkMode can throw IOException across the
  module boundary (AGENTS §3 sealed results). Fix: add `.catch` on the data flows; wrap edits in BudsResult.
- D-12 nit — CodecRouter: SubscribeToSettingsChanges error packets (CLIENT_/SERVER_ERROR) are not in `answersUs` → reported as UnidentifiedFrame; the
  "Settings stream subscribed" log is printed on send success only, so a Buds-side refusal of the subscription is never named. Fix: route an error for
  METHOD_SUBSCRIBE_TO_SETTINGS_CHANGES to a log line "settings stream refused (status)".
- D-13 nit — MessageStreamFrameSplitter: `ArrayList<Byte>` + `repeat(totalLength){ buffer.removeAt(0) }` is O(n²) per chunk (boxed bytes). Small frames,
  so harmless; ByteArray + offset would be cleaner.
- D-14 nit (dead code, intentional) — DLCI 0x08 path (Dlci.GSND_CONTROL, gsndSplitter, CaseBatteryFrameDecoder, RoutedFrame.CaseBattery → Unit) never fed
  (ADR-043); SoftwareInfo.firmwareStrings is unused in main (KDoc says "the Safe-Mode gate checks" it — the router calls entries().map{}.distinct()). Fix:
  drop firmwareStrings or have routeMaestro use it; correct the KDoc.
- No leftovers of ADR-061/062/063 code in :data main (grep hold/reclaim: only comments saying they are gone; DarkModeSettingsStore documents the ignored
  `hold_reclaim_mode` key). Constants match ARCHITECTURE.md §3.2 (all 15 BudsRepositoryImpl constants + SessionReopener's two).
- TODO(verify): EqFrame.kt:39 (16 vs 18 semantics, ADR-020 open item) and CaseBatteryFrame.kt:53 (fresh flag, ADR-014 🟡) — both still describe open
  hypotheses; the second guards a decoder whose channel is never opened.

#### Tests: SettingsStreamCodecTest, SettingsStreamFixtures, BudsRepositoryImplTest 3078–3592 read
- D-15 minor — SettingsStreamFixtures.kt `SET_TRANSPARENT_CAP051_1527 = "0812001401e8e880" + 16×"00"` is labelled as CAP-051 frame 1527 but the real frame
  (tshark -r CAP-051-btsnoop_hci.log -Y frame.number==1527 → exit 0) is `0812001401e8e880e4e68882be02fffe63abde07ef49dae8` (the official app's
  nonce+MAC). Not real bytes (AGENTS §11); it is also unused. Fix: replace with the real bytes or delete.
- D-16 minor — fuzz `fuzzed stream packets never throw` (SettingsStreamCodecTest) and CodecRouter-based fuzzing: `feedCounting` passes no `onDecoderFault`;
  CodecRouter.decodeGuarded catches RuntimeException and reports it as malformed, so a decoder that throws still passes the test ("never throw" is not
  asserted). Also `assertTrue(mode == null || mode in AncMode.entries)` is always true by type. Fix: count onDecoderFault and assert 0; drop the tautology.
- D-17 minor — BudsRepositoryImplTest `the automatic re-open subscribes to the settings stream again` (~3244–3280): the re-open's snapshot Get is answered
  by autoAck's OFF (Cap065 6334) while the 4:13 read answers ADAPTIVE (1225) — two sources disagree in one session, which never happens on hardware
  (CAP-076 §6.2: Notify before 4:13, same mode, 12/12). The CHANGED_SINCE_LAST_READ assertion passes only because of that, masking D-01. Fix: answer
  the Get with an ADAPTIVE Notify (CAP-076 H1 4094 → 4135 order) and assert CHANGED_SINCE_LAST_READ survives the equal read.
- D-18 nit — `a differing read of 13 …` asserts `READ` for "the same mode read again" — will need to change with D-01's fix (keep cause on equal read).
- D-19 minor — no test for D-02's interleaving (the Get's Notify reaching _ancOutcomes after the Set's wait subscribed); with StandardTestDispatcher the
  router finishes handleRoutedFrame before the waiter resumes, so it cannot occur. Fix: a test that emits the Get's Notify, then the Set's wait subscribes,
  then emits an extra Notified/close — or unit-test the "skip Notified when answersGet" rule directly.
- D-20 nit — `the stream packet … set by this app`/same-mode tests never assert the log text of a same-mode push (D-03 uncaught).
- D-21 nit — ancSetOnClaim sets `lastSetMode/lastSetAt` before the send and never clears them on a NAK (Rejected) or SendFailed; a hold to that same mode
  within 2 s after a refused Set is classed SET_BY_APP. Fix: clear lastSetMode on Rejected/SendFailed.
- Settings-stream decoding IS fuzz-covered (SettingsStreamCodecTest `fuzzed stream packets never throw`, 8 400 mutations + prefixes; field 13 0–255 sweep).

#### OversizedLengthTest, CodecRouterTest, test-name sweep done
- D-16 also applies to OversizedLengthTest `mutated real frames with a valid CRC never throw` and CodecRouterTest `random chunks never throw` (router guard
  swallows a throw into onMalformed; the tests do not pass onDecoderFault). CodecRouterTest random chunks (≤49 B, random) almost never pass the HDLC CRC, so
  they exercise splitters only.
- D-22 minor — applyAncState does `_ancModeFresh.tryEmit(mode)` for stream packets and 4:13 answers; refreshAncMode (manual refresh, Refresh battery,
  Connect snapshot) waits on _ancModeFresh, so a MAESTRO-channel packet can end the Get's wait; getPending is then cleared in finally, and the Get's real
  Notify arriving later is classified as unsolicited (CHANGED_BY_BUDS if it differs). Fix: emit _ancModeFresh only from the DLCI 0x04 Notify.
- D-23 nit — BudsRepositoryImplTest 3097–3170: section/DisplayNames still say "ADR-062 Update 2 … a re-claim's Get answer", "Notify on the held claim" —
  describe the removed re-claim/hold; rename to ADR-065 item 4 wording.
- D-24 nit — CodecRouterTest `field 18 is flagged as persisted` builds the frame with EqFrameEncoder (hand-built) without the "synthetic, labelled" note
  required by AGENTS §11; SettingsStreamCodecTest `an unread field …` DisplayName lists (10, 20, 31) but loops over 8, 10, 20, 31.
DONE.

### sub_hw_ui

#### sub_hw_ui findings (in progress)
#### Read: AndroidManifest.xml, app/build.gradle.kts, AncTileService.kt, BudsForegroundService.kt, BluetoothStateObserver.kt
- Manifest: perms OK (CONNECT, POST_NOTIFICATIONS, FGS, FGS_CONNECTED_DEVICE); no INTERNET; no BLUETOOTH_SCAN (AGENTS §2 lists it but it was removed by 0044 APP-13 — doc drift in AGENTS.md §2 "Minimal permission set" still lists BLUETOOTH_SCAN). Note: <service BudsForegroundService> not in app manifest -> check hardware manifest.
- AncTileService:147 `tile.label = "ANC"` hard-coded vs manifest @string/tile_anc_label (nit, i18n/consistency).
- AncTileService:136-141 toast only on BudsResult.Failure; CAP-076 §5.3 silent tap: success returned if Set's wait takes Get's Notify (root in :data BudsRepositoryImpl ~973/993). Tile cannot detect; fix in :data (ignore Notify received before Set written) — out of my files but tile has no fallback.
- AncTileService:118-127 tap when not Ready: startActivityAndCollapse(PendingIntent) fine on API34; no isLocked/unlockAndRun handling (on lock screen tap launches activity behind keyguard - system prompts; minor).
- BudsForegroundService:221-226 getLaunchIntentForPackage may return null -> PendingIntent.getActivity(null) NPE (nit, practically never).
- BluetoothStateObserver:304 registerReceiver without RECEIVER_* flag: OK for protected system broadcast on targetSdk 34 (lint UnspecifiedRegisterReceiverFlag may warn; nit).
#### Read: hardware manifest, BleLogger, BudsTransport, RfcommSocket, ProfileProxies, BluetoothPermissions, BudsSdpUuids, RfcommBudsTransport
- hardware manifest: FGS declared connectedDevice, exported=false. OK.
- BleLogger: 20_000 cap OK; Log.d for events (below INFO) OK; describe() redacts MAC. OK.
- RfcommBudsTransport:336-341 check-then-put race: closeAll() (reportLoss from another reader) between line 336 check and line 340 put leaves aux socket open forever (auxSockets.clear already ran; reader launched in cancelled scope never runs) -> leaked DLCI 0x04 port, the exact collision ai-sessions/0039 warns about. Fix: do the check+put under `lock`, and re-check isOpen after put (close if closed).
- RfcommBudsTransport:328 openChannel has no per-channel guard against two concurrent opens (both pass containsKey) -> second connect() collides & kills incumbent. Verify caller serializes (data layer mutex); else add a Mutex.
#### Read: BudsCompanionPairing, OsConnectionObserver, LinkEvaluation, ConnectionStateMachine, PairingLogic
- BudsCompanionPairing:177 manager.associate(...) not wrapped in try/catch (AGENTS §8): a RuntimeException (IllegalStateException e.g. missing feature / SecurityException) leaves associationInFlight=true forever (every later tap -> AlreadyInProgress) and propagates. Fix: try/catch RuntimeException -> associationInFlight.set(false); onFailure(AssociationFailed(describe(e))).
- BudsCompanionPairing:364 NAME_PATTERN `(?i).*Pixel\\s*Buds.*` matches ARCH §9.0a. OK. TODO(verify) comments at 51-53 and 144-145 now stale (verified on hardware since 0036/0041) - nit.
- OsConnectionObserver:130-139: proxies requested only if permission held at collection start. If BLUETOOTH_CONNECT is granted later while the flow keeps running, evaluate() has requested={} and bound={} -> LinkEvaluation.evaluate returns NOT_CONNECTED (containsAll(emptySet)=true) even when Android has the Buds connected, until the flow is re-collected. Verify whether MainActivity re-collects on permission grant. Fix: in evaluate, if requested is empty and permission now ok, request proxies (or return null/UNKNOWN when requested is empty).
#### Read: MainActivity, RepositoryModule, TransportModule, AppUiSession, OpenControlApplication
- RETRACT OsConnectionObserver permission item: MainActivity:367 re-creates the observer on a Bluetooth-grant change (remember(permissionState.bluetoothConnect.isGranted)). Only a nit: the dependency is implicit.
- **MAJOR** MainActivity:143 `private val companionPairing by lazy { BudsCompanionPairing(this) }` is a second instance besides the @Singleton one (RepositoryModule:72) that the repository (bondedDeviceProvider) and AppUiSession use. "Use different Buds" (MainActivity:273 forgetAssociations) sets `chooseAgainRequested` (in-memory, per instance, BudsCompanionPairing:67) only on the Activity's instance -> (a) repository.connect() via singleton still name-falls-back to the old single bonded "Pixel Buds"; (b) any Activity recreation (rotation, theme/locale/config change) creates a new instance with chooseAgainRequested=false, so the lookup finds the old Buds by name again and the "choose again" state is lost. Fix: inject the singleton BudsCompanionPairing in MainActivity (associate() works with app context + main executor; the IntentSender is still launched by the Activity) or move chooseAgainRequested to a shared/persisted place.
- MainActivity:217 Toast.makeText(this@MainActivity, …) from applicationScope after an async write: Activity may be destroyed (rotation) -> leaked Activity reference / toast on dead context. Use applicationContext.
- MainActivity:258-262 orphaned KDoc ("The pairing entry point behind Pair a device…") sits above bondedChanged's KDoc, not above startPairing (line 280). nit.
- MainActivity:482-486 ACTION_REQUEST_ENABLE: SecurityException only logged, no user-visible text (silent tap) when Nearby devices permission missing. minor.
- MainActivity:507 / AncTileService:147 tile label "ANC" hard-coded twice; manifest uses @string/tile_anc_label. nit.
- OpenControlApplication:96 notification "Connected — ANC: ${it.name}" exposes the enum constant name (e.g. NOISE_CANCELLATION) instead of the UI label. nit/minor.
- OpenControlApplication:71-86: text updates re-call startForegroundService while app is in background (ANC push changes text) — may throw ForegroundServiceStartNotAllowedException (caught) leaving stale notification text; acceptable but the log line will say "not started" though service is running. nit.
- BudsForegroundService/manifest: START_NOT_STICKY + connectedDevice OK (ARCH §6.0a).
#### Read: all domain/main (17 files). AGPL headers present in all .kt of hardware/domain/app/ui (grep). No .java sources.
- AncTile.kt:42 tile subtitle = enum name title-cased -> "Active" for AncMode.ACTIVE, while the class KDoc/AncTileService call it "Noise cancelling" and the app UI labels it differently (check AncScreen). minor UI consistency; use the same label function as the ANC screen.

Fix: one `ancModeLabel(mode)` from ANC_MODE_LIST_ORDER used by all four. minor.
